# Small-M B x Q acceptance (SM2)

Generated by `linear_smallm_table.py --bxq-markdown` from the current acceptance logs (20 rotating-weight pairs per row; epsilon = p95 of F.linear self-pairs). Comparator: F.linear (cuBLAS), the kernel vLLM 0.22.1 and SGLang dispatch for these BF16 linears on sm120/sm121, timed with torch's default reduced-precision BF16 reduction (as both stacks and the engine run it). CuTe is dispatched only where the measured configuration reached paired speedup >= 1.03 above its epsilon at every measured M of its M bucket, both bucket edges included; every other row runs F.linear (speedup 1 by construction); a row whose measured CuTe configuration is not in the table is listed as an F.linear fallback with the CuTe ratio it measured. SOL = 2(NK + MK + MN) bytes / read-only DRAM ceiling (1661 / 1543 / 199 GB/s); SOL % uses the dispatched time. GB10 (secondary) is measured at decode M only. Rows and round sums both use the cold-L2 (rotating-weight) timings, the regime the engine runs: a decode step or DFlash round streams every weight once, far beyond L2. Engine re-pairs (consecutive calls, weights alternating cold/hot in L2) must pass at both bucket edges, hot-L2 re-pairs only veto; neither is listed.
